<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا حَاتِمُ بْنُ بَكْرٍ الضَّبِّيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ يَعْلَى، زُنْبُورٌ حَدَّثَنَا عَنْبَسَةُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أُمِّ سَلَمَةَ، قَالَتْ: نَهَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ عَنِ الْقُنُوتِ فِي الْفَجْرِ ‏.‏</p>

## Narration 1243

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-ibnmajah%3A1243)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا نَصْرُ بْنُ عَلِيٍّ الْجَهْضَمِيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا يَزِيدُ بْنُ زُرَيْعٍ، حَدَّثَنَا هِشَامٌ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ كَانَ يَقْنُتُ فِي صَلاَةِ الصُّبْحِ. يَدْعُو عَلَى حَىٍّ مِنْ أَحْيَاءِ الْعَرَبِ، شَهْرًا. ثُمَّ تَرَكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1243]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1243" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Ibn Majah · 1243</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Ibn Majah   1243 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from Anas bin Malik that the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) used to recite Qunut in the Subh prayer, and he used to supplicate in it against one of the Arab tribes for a month, then he stopped doing so</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1243]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1243" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अनस बिन मालिक (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) नमाज़-ए-फ़ज्र में दुआ-ए-क़ुनूत पढ़ते रहे, और अरब के एक क़बीले (क़बीला-ए-मुज़र) पर एक महीने तक बद-दुआ करते रहे, फिर इसे आप ने तर्क कर दिया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1243]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1243" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">انس بن مالک رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نماز فجر میں دعائے قنوت پڑھتے رہے، اور عرب کے ایک قبیلہ ( قبیلہ مضر پر ) ایک مہینہ تک بد دعا کرتے رہے، پھر اسے آپ نے ترک کر دیا ۱؎۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1243]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1243" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Anas bin Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) namaz-e-fajr mein dua-e-qunoot padhte rahe, aur Arab ke ek qabeele (Qabeela Mudar) par ek mahine tak bad-dua karte rahe, phir ise aap ne tark kar diya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1243]</p>

## Narration 1244

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1244" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ لَمَّا رَفَعَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ رَأْسَهُ مِنْ صَلاَةِ الصُّبْحِ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ اللَّهُمَّ أَنْجِ الْوَلِيدَ بْنَ الْوَلِيدِ، وَسَلَمَةَ بْنَ هِشَامٍ، وَعَيَّاشَ بْنَ أَبِي رَبِيعَةَ، وَالْمُسْتَضْعَفِينَ بِمَكَّةَ. اللَّهُمَّ اشْدُدْ وَطْأَتَكَ عَلَى مُضَرَ، وَاجْعَلْهَا عَلَيْهِمْ سِنِينَ كَسِنِي يُوسُفَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1244" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic supplication report. Attribution: Abu Hurairah. The complete English field explicitly reports the Prophet’s supplication after bowing in Subh, including the named persons and Mudar. The historical prayer is preserved exactly as attributed by this source.</p>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Abu Hurairah said:“When the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) raised his head from Ruku’ in the Subh prayer, he said: ‘O Allah, save Al-Walid bin Walid, Salamah bin Hisham and ‘Ayyash bin Abu Rabi’ah, and the oppressed in Makkah. O Allah, tighten Your grip on Mudar, and send them years of famine like the famine of Yusuf.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1244]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1244" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic supplication. Attribution: Abu Hurairah reporting the Fajr supplication. Read both full fields. The Arabic/transliterated formula and its complete supplied own-language meaning belong to the reported prayer; Hindi parenthesizes that translation and Urdu parenthesizes the entire field. Those are not blanket-excluded as commentary. Preserve all names, Mudar/Muzar spelling and the famine comparison as supplied.</p>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं के जब रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) नमाज़-ए-फ़ज्र से अपना सर उठाते तो फ़रमाते: अल्लाहुम्म अन्जी अल-वलीद बिन अल-वलीद व सलमा बिन हिशाम व &#x27;अय्याश बिन अबी रबी&#x27;अह वल-मुस्तद&#x27;अफ़ीना बी मक्काह. अल्लाहुम्माश-दुद वत&#x27;अतका &#x27;अला मुज़र वज&#x27;अलहा &#x27;अलैहिम सिनेन क-सिनेन यूसुफ़. (ऐ अल्लाह! वलीद बिन वलीद, सलमा बिन हिशाम, &#x27;अय्याश बिन अबी रबी&#x27;अह और मक्का के कमज़ोर हाल मुसलमानों को निजात दे दे, ऐ अल्लाह! तू अपनी पकड़ क़बीला मुज़र पर सख़्त कर दे, और यूसुफ़ (अलैहिस्सलाम) के अहद के क़हत के सालों की तरह इन पर भी क़हत मुसल्लत कर दे)।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1244]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1244" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="rtl">(ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ جب رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نماز فجر سے اپنا سر اٹھاتے تو فرماتے: «اللهم أنج الوليد بن الوليد وسلمة بن هشام وعياش بن أبي ربيعة والمستضعفين بمكة اللهم اشدد وطأتك على مضر واجعلها عليهم سنين كسني يوسف» اے اللہ! ولید بن ولید، سلمہ بن ہشام، عیاش بن ابی ربیعہ اور مکہ کے کمزور حال مسلمانوں کو نجات دیدے، اے اللہ! تو اپنی پکڑ قبیلہ مضر پر سخت کر دے، اور یوسف علیہ السلام کے عہد کے قحط کے سالوں کی طرح ان پر بھی قحط مسلط کر دے ) </p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="rtl">۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1244]</p>

## Narration 1245

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-ibnmajah%3A1245)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، وَمُحَمَّدُ بْنُ الصَّبَّاحِ، قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ مَعْمَرٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ أَبِي كَثِيرٍ، عَنْ ضَمْضَمِ بْنِ جَوْسٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ أَمَرَ بِقَتْلِ الأَسْوَدَيْنِ فِي الصَّلاَةِ الْعَقْرَبِ وَالْحَيَّةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1245]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1245" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from Abu Hurairah that the Prophet (ﷺ) commanded killing the two black ones during prayer; the scorpion and the snake</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1245]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1245" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1246

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ عُثْمَانَ بْنِ حَكِيمٍ الأَوْدِيُّ، وَالْعَبَّاسُ بْنُ جَعْفَرٍ، قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا عَلِيُّ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ الدَّهَّانُ، حَدَّثَنَا الْحَكَمُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، قَالَتْ لَدَغَتِ النَّبِيَّ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ عَقْرَبٌ وَهُوَ فِي الصَّلاَةِ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لَعَنَ اللَّهُ الْعَقْرَبَ مَا تَدَعُ الْمُصَلِّيَ وَغَيْرَ الْمُصَلِّي اقْتُلُوهَا فِي الْحِلِّ وَالْحَرَمِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English source independently read. The local report explicitly describes the Prophet’s speech, action, approval, practice, or physical description. The observed publisher or later-speaker tail is retained separately as source context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that ‘Aishah said:“The Prophet (ﷺ) was stung by a scorpion while he was performing prayer, and he said: ‘May Allah curse the scorpion, for it does not spare anyone, whether he is praying or not. Kill them whether you are in Ihram or not.’”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1246]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Ibn Majah   1246 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उम्म-उल-मोमिनीन आयशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) कहती हैं के नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को एक बिच्छू ने नमाज़ में डंक मार दिया, नमाज़ से फ़ारिग़ हो कर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अल्लाह बिच्छू पर लानत करे के वोह नमाज़ी ओ ग़ैर-नमाज़ी किसी को नहीं छोड़ता, इसे हरम और हरम से बाहर, हर जगह में मार डालो।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1246" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1247

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ يَحْيَى، حَدَّثَنَا الْهَيْثَمُ بْنُ جَمِيلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مَنْدَلٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي رَافِعٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ جَدِّهِ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ قَتَلَ عَقْرَبًا وَهُوَ فِي الصَّلاَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1247]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1247" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<p dir="auto">अबू रफ़ी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने नमाज़ की हालत में एक बिच्छू को मार डाला.</p>

## Narration 1248

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-ibnmajah%3A1248)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ نُمَيْرٍ، وَأَبُو أُسَامَةَ عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، عَنْ خُبَيْبِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ حَفْصِ بْنِ عَاصِمٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ نَهَى عَنْ صَلاَتَيْنِ عَنِ الصَّلاَةِ بَعْدَ الْفَجْرِ حَتَّى تَطْلُعَ الشَّمْسُ وَبَعْدَ الْعَصْرِ حَتَّى تَغْرُبَ الشَّمْسُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1248]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1248" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Ibn Majah   1248 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from Abu Hurairah that the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) forbade two prayers:prayer after the Fajr until the sun has risen, and prayer after ‘Asr until the sun has set</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1248]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1248" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने दो नमाज़ों से मना फ़रमाया, एक फ़ज्र के बाद नमाज़ पढ़ने से सूरज निकलने तक, दूसरे असर के बाद नमाज़ पढ़ने से सूरज डूब जाने तक।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1248]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1248" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے دو نمازوں سے منع فرمایا، ایک فجر کے بعد نماز پڑھنے سے سورج نکلنے تک، دوسرے عصر کے بعد نماز پڑھنے سے سورج ڈوب جانے تک ۱؎۔</p>

## Narration 1249

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-ibnmajah%3A1249)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 5</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ يَعْلَى التَّيْمِيُّ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ بْنِ عُمَيْرٍ، عَنْ قَزَعَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ صَلاَةَ بَعْدَ الْعَصْرِ حَتَّى تَغْرُبَ الشَّمْسُ وَلاَ صَلاَةَ بَعْدَ الْفَجْرِ حَتَّى تَطْلُعَ الشَّمْسُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1249]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1249" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Ibn Majah · 1249</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Ibn Majah   1249 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from Abu Sa’eed Al-Khudri that the Prophet (ﷺ) said:“There is no prayer after the ‘Asr until the sun has set, and there is no prayer after the Fajr until the sun has risen.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1249]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1249" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू सईद खुदरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: नमाज़-इ-असर के बाद कोई नमाज़ नहीं यहाँ तक के सूरज डूब जाये और नमाज़-इ-फज्र के बाद कोई नमाज़ नहीं यहाँ तक के सूरज निकल आये.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1249]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1249" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Saeed Khudri (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain ke Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Namaz-e-asr ke baad koi namaz nahi yahan tak ke suraj doob jaye aur namaz-e-fajr ke baad koi namaz nahi yahan tak ke suraj nikal aaye.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1249]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ بَشَّارٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ جَعْفَرٍ، حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا أَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا عَفَّانُ، حَدَّثَنَا هَمَّامٌ، حَدَّثَنَا قَتَادَةُ، عَنْ أَبِي الْعَالِيَةِ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ شَهِدَ عِنْدِي رِجَالٌ مَرْضِيُّونَ فِيهِمْ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ وَأَرْضَاهُمْ عِنْدِي عُمَرُ أَنَّ رَ سُولَ اللَّهِ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ صَلاَةَ بَعْدَ الْفَجْرِ حَتَّى تَطْلُعَ الشَّمْسُ وَلاَ صَلاَةَ بَعْدَ الْعَصْرِ حَتَّى تَغْرُبَ الشَّمْسُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1250" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Ibn ‘Abbas said:“Good men among whom was ‘Umar bin Khattab, and the best of them in my view is ‘Umar, testified before me that the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) said: ‘There is no prayer after Fajr until the sun has risen, and there is no prayer after the ‘Asr until the sun has set.’”</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/1250" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic statement report. Attribution: Ibn Abbas, citing Umar among multiple witnesses. <p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुम) कहते हैं के मेरे पास कई अच्छे लोगों ने गवाही दी, उन में मेरे नज़दीक सब से ज़्यादा अच्छे शख़्स उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) थे के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: नमाज़-ए-फ़ज्र के बाद कोई नमाज़ नहीं यहाँ तक के सूरज निकल आए, और असर के बाद कोई नमाज़ नहीं यहाँ तक के सूरज डूब जाए।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1250]</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source excerpt 1</p>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما کہتے ہیں کہ میرے پاس کئی اچھے لوگوں نے گواہی دی، ان میں میرے نزدیک سب زیادہ اچھے شخص عمر رضی اللہ عنہ تھے </p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source excerpt 2</p>

<p dir="rtl">کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: نماز فجر کے بعد کوئی نماز نہیں یہاں تک کہ سورج نکل آئے، اور عصر کے بعد کوئی نماز نہیں یہاں تک کہ سورج ڈوب جائے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 1250]</p>
